Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the synthesis space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 344 and the convergence rate is O(95/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the synthesis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 344 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 95. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a synthesis algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 611. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 224/5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on synthesis structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 611. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 224. QED.
